'{"chunk_id":"2f8c7f","wall_time_seconds":10.0097651,"session_id":42083,"original_token_count":3012,"output":"import { useMemo, useState } from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { useNavigate } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\r\\nimport { Send } from \\"lucide-react\\";\\r\\nimport { useUserPermissions } from \\"@/hooks/useUserPermissions\\";\\r\\nimport { AssignWorkSheet, type AssignWorkSheetItem } from \\"@/components/teacher/library/AssignWorkSheet\\";\\r\\nimport { useAdminActivities, useStudentActivities } from \\"@/features/activity-centre\\";\\r\\nimport type { Activity, ActivityAssignmentSummary } from \\"@/features/activity-centre\\";\\r\\nimport { ActivityCard } from \\"@/features/activity-centre/components/ActivityCard\\";\\r\\nimport { librarySortIndex } from \\"@/features/activity-centre/content\\";\\r\\nimport { slugFromTitle } from \\"@/features/activity-centre/worlds/registry\\";\\r\\nimport \\"@/features/activity-centre/activity-centre.css\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The Activity Centre.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * For a student this is the list of Activities assigned to them, ordered by the\\r\\n * teaching progression: the scope of text each skill works on, a word up to a\\r\\n * passage. It used to sort by status and then by seed date, which put the\\r\\n * passage-level Activity first and the word-level one near the bottom, the\\r\\n * curriculum\'s own argument backwards.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * For an admin it is also the library. Assignment-gating is right for students\\r\\n * and was wrong for the person doing the assigning: the Centre showed him \\"No\\r\\n * activities assigned yet\\" while eight authored Activities sat in the table,\\r\\n * with the only way to open or assign one being the Teacher Desk. The library\\r\\n * below is admin-only, opens each Activity through the row-free preview, and\\r\\n * assigns through the same sheet every other assign surface uses.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Each card carries the world it opens into. Eight Activities drawn as eight\\r\\n * identical rows read as one undifferentiated list, which is the wrong\\r\\n * impression when every one of them is a different place.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function ActivityCentre() {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const { assignments, loading, error } = useStudentActivities();\\r\\n  const { is_admin } = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Her activities, which is not the same list as her assignment rows.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * Starting a section builds an assignment of its own -- it has to, because\\r\\n   * the row carries the Details her Coverage is scored against and cannot be\\r\\n   * written by the client (`start_section_attempt`). Listing those rows here\\r\\n   * turned one drill into a card per run: three sections done and Read, Recall,\\r\\n   * Colour appeared four times, as though Joshua had assigned it four times.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * An Attempt is not something she was assigned. It is a run of something she\\r\\n   * was assigned, and it already has a home on the door\'s section list, where\\r\\n   * it opens its own Report Card. The door is the card; the runs are inside it.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * Filtered here rather than in `listStudentAssignments`, because the door\\r\\n   * reads the same hook and needs exactly the rows this page is dropping.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const ordered = useMemo(\\r\\n    () =>\\r\\n      assignments\\r\\n        .filter((a) => a.mode !== \\"section\\")\\r\\n        .sort(\\r\\n          (a, b) => librarySortIndex(slugFromTitle(a.title)) - librarySortIndex(slugFromTitle(b.title)),\\r\\n        ),\\r\\n    [assignments],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The door is already on her list when the library was assigned to her, and\\r\\n   * that card leads to the same place. Only the unassigned case needs a way in.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const hasDoorAssignment = useMemo(\\r\\n    () => ordered.some((a) => a.mode === \\"section-library\\"),\\r\\n    [ordered],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    // No back link of its own: the classroom rail is the way back, and it was\\r\\n    // pointing at /foyer, which only redirects to /classroom anyway. One name\\r\\n    // for this surface too, not \\"Activity Centre\\" over \\"Your activities\\".\\r\\n    <div className=\\"activity-centre\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"mx-auto max-w-5xl\\">\\r\\n        <header className=\\"max-w-2xl\\">\\r\\n          <h1 className=\\"ac-display text-4xl text-foreground\\">Activities</h1>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"mt-2 text-base leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n            Coach-led skill exercises, ordered by how much text each one works on: a single word\\r\\n            first, a whole passage last.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        </header>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-8\\">\\r\\n          {loading && (\\r\\n            <div className=\\"flex justify-center py-12\\">\\r\\n              <div className=\\"h-6 w-6 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-[hsl(var(--muted))] border-t-[hsl(var(--ac-emerald))]\\" />\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n          )}\\r\\n\\r\\n          {!loading && error && (\\r\\n            <p className=\\"rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n              Could not load your activities: {error}\\r\\n            </p>\\r\\n          )}\\r\\n\\r\\n          {/* An admin with nothing assigned is not an empty Centre, so the\\r\\n              student\'s empty state would be telling him to wait for himself. */}\\r\\n          {!loading && !error && ordered.length === 0 && !is_admin && (\\r\\n            <div className=\\"rounded-[24px] border border-dashed border-border bg-card p-10 text-center\\">\\r\\n              <p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n                No activities assigned yet. Your instructor will add them here.\\r\\n              </p>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n          )}\\r\\n\\r\\n          {ordered.length > 0 && (\\r\\n            <div className=\\"grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2\\">\\r\\n              {ordered.map((a, i) => (\\r\\n                <ActivityCard\\r\\n                  key={a.id}\\r\\n                  assignment={a}\\r\\n                  position={i + 1}\\r\\n                  onOpen={() => navigate(`/activities/${a.id}`)}\\r\\n                />\\r\\n              ))}\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n          )}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n        {/*\\r\\n          * Read, Recall, Colour is open to anyone with Activities.\\r\\n          *\\r\\n          * Every section behind this door is startable on demand -- the Attempt\\r\\n          * is built server-side when she picks one -- so gating the door itself\\r\\n          * behind an assignment only hid a drill she was already allowed to\\r\\n          * run. It stays visible whether or not the library was assigned.\\r\\n          */}\\r\\n        {!hasDoorAssignment && (\\r\\n        <section className=\\"mt-10\\">\\r\\n          <button\\r\\n            type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n            onClick={() => navigate(\\"/activities/sections\\")}\\r\\n            className=\\"w-full rounded-[24px] border border-border bg-card p-6 text-left transition-colors hover:border-foreground/30\\"\\r\\n          >\\r\\n            <span className=\\"text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n              Out loud\\r\\n            </span>\\r\\n            <h2 className=\\"ac-display mt-2 text-2xl text-foreground\\">Read, Recall, Colour</h2>\\r\\n            <p className=\\"mt-1.5 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n              Say each argument back from memory, one whole section in a single take. Choose any\\r\\n              section and start it now — every attempt is kept.\\r\\n            </p>\\r\\n            <span className=\\"mt-4 inline-flex items-center text-sm font-semibold text-foreground underline underline-offset-4\\">\\r\\n              Choose a section\\r\\n            </span>\\r\\n          </button>\\r\\n        </section>\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n\\r\\n        {is_admin && <AdminLibrary assignedCount={ordered.length} />}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Every authored Activity, openable and assignable, for the one person who\\r\\n * needs to see the library rather than a list of what he has been given.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Opening runs the preview route, which works off the Activity DEFINITION and\\r\\n * writes nothing: looking at an Activity must never put it on his own\\r\\n * Classroom. Assigning goes through AssignWorkSheet, the single assign pipeline\\r\\n * shared with drills and homework, so an Activity can ship in a mixed bundle as\\r\\n * one assignment event rather than as its own separate ping.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction AdminLibrary({ assignedCount }: { assignedCount: number }) {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const { activities, loading, error } = useAdminActivities();\\r\\n  const [sheetItems, setSheetItems] = useState<AssignWorkSheetItem[] | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const ordered = useMemo(\\r\\n    () => [...activities].sort((a, b) => librarySortIndex(a.slug) - librarySortIndex(b.slug)),\\r\\n    [activities],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <section className=\\"mt-12 border-t border-border pt-8\\">\\r\\n      <header className=\\"max-w-2xl\\">\\r\\n        <h2 className=\\"ac-display text-2xl text-foreground\\">The library</h2>\\r\\n        <p className=\\"mt-1.5 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n          {assignedCount > 0\\r\\n            ? \\"Every Activity you can assign. Open one to work it yourself; nothing you open here is recorded.\\"\\r\\n            : \\"Every Activity you can assign. Open one to work it yourself, or send it to a student. Nothing you open here is recorded.\\"}\\r\\n        </p>\\r\\n      </header>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {loading && (\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-6 flex justify-center py-8\\">\\r\\n          <div className=\\"h-6 w-6 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-[hsl(var(--muted))] border-t-[hsl(var(--ac-emerald))]\\" />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n\\r\\n      {!loading && error && (\\r\\n        <p className=\\"mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n          Could not load the library: {error}\\r\\n        </p>\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* Zero rows here is a real failure, not an empty library: the Activities\\r\\n          are seeded. It usually means this admin account cannot read the table. */}\\r\\n      {!loading && !error && ordered.length === 0 && (\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-6 rounded-[24px] border border-dashed border-border bg-card p-10 text-center\\">\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n            No Activities came back. They are seeded, so this is a read failing rather than an empty\\r\\n            library.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n\\r\\n      <div className=\\"mt-6 grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2\\">\\r\\n        {ordered.map((activity, i) => (\\r\\n          <div key={activity.id} className=\\"flex flex-col gap-2\\">\\r\\n            <ActivityCard\\r\\n              assignment={asCard(activity)}\\r\\n              position={i + 1}\\r\\n              onOpen={() => navigate(`/activities/preview/${activity.id}`)}\\r\\n            />\\r\\n            <button\\r\\n              type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n              onClick={() =>\\r\\n                setSheetItems([\\r\\n                  {\\r\\n                    item: { kind: \\"activity\\", activityId: activity.id },\\r\\n                    title: activity.title,\\r\\n                    kindLabel: \\"Activity\\",\\r\\n                  },\\r\\n                ])\\r\\n              }\\r\\n              className=\\"inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 self-start rounded-full border border-border bg-card px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-foreground/30 hover:text-foreground\\"\\r\\n            >\\r\\n              <Send className=\\"h-3 w-3\\" />\\r\\n              Assign {activity.title}\\r\\n            </button>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        ))}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <AssignWorkSheet\\r\\n        open={!!sheetItems}\\r\\n        items={sheetItems ?? []}\\r\\n        onClose={() => setSheetItems(null)}\\r\\n      />\\r\\n    </section>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * An Activity definition in the shape the card draws.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The card is written against an assignment because that is what a student\\r\\n * holds, and the two carry the same copy: title, subtitle, items. The status\\r\\n * fields are the honest defaults for a definition nobody has been given -- no\\r\\n * progress, no score -- so the card shows the item count and nothing it cannot\\r\\n * back up.\\r\\n */\\r\\n/** A definition dressed as the list row a card draws. */\\r\\nfunction asCard(activity: Activity): ActivityAssignmentSummary {\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    id: activity.id,\\r\\n    activity_id: activity.id,\\r\\n    student_id: \\"\\",\\r\\n    title: activity.title,\\r\\n    subtitle: activity.subtitle,\\r\\n    instructions: activity.instructions,\\r\\n    status: \\"assigned\\",\\r\\n    score: null,\\r\\n    started_at: null,\\r\\n    completed_at: null,\\r\\n    created_at: activity.created_at,\\r\\n    item_count: activity.items.length,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"f3ed2c","wall_time_seconds":10.0006194,"session_id":73576,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

Check device storage before starting.\\"); }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  const returnToHub = () => { setParams({}); setMode(null); setReviewId(null); };\\r\\n  const review = runs.find(r => r.id === reviewId);\\r\\n  const current = run?.questions[run.position];\\r\\n  const questionAnswers = run && current ? \\"Answer revealed\\" : selected!.text,\\r\\n      verdict: reveal ? null : selected!.id === current.correctChoiceId ? \\"correct\\" as const : \\"incorrect\\" as const,\\r\\n      attempt: questionAnswers.length + 1, revealed: reveal, at: new Date().toISOString() };\\r\\n    update({ answers: [...run.answers, answer], stage: \\"feedback\\" });\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  return <main className=\\"conditional-room activity-centre\\">\\r\\n    <div className=\\"conditional-shell\\">\\r\\n      <header className=\\"conditional-topbar\\">\\r\\n        {run || mode || review ? <button onClick={returnToHub} className=\\"conditional-back\\"><ArrowLeft size={16} /> Conditional worlds</button>\\r\\n          : <Link to=\\"/activities\\" className=\\"conditional-back\\"><ArrowLeft size={16} /> Activities</Link>}\\r\\n        <div className=\\"conditional-save\\" role=\\"status\\">{saveStatus}\\r\\n          {!localOnly && saveStatus.includes(\\"unavailable\\") && <button onClick={() => void refresh()}>Retry sync</button>}</div>\\r\\n      </header>\\r\\n      {storageError && <p role=\\"alert\\" className=\\"conditional-error\\">{storageError}</p>}\\r\\n      {!loaded ? Work through every supplied exercise, then try more complex connections.\\" },\\r\\n  { id: \\"must-be-true\\", title: \\"Must Be True\\", icon: Check, description: \\"Use the rules and facts to identify what must follow, including and/or conditions.\\" },\\r\\n] as const;\\r\\nconst titleFor = (mode: PracticeMode) => WORLDS.find(w => w.id === mode)?.title ?? mode;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function ConditionalPractice() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const { is_admin } = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const { assignments, loading, error } = useStudentActivities();\\r\\n  const [params] = useSearchParams();\\r\\n  const general = assignments.find(a => a.activity_id === GENERAL_ID && a.id === params.get(\\"assignment\\"))\\r\\n    ?? assignments.find(a => a.activity_id === GENERAL_ID);\\r\\n  const preview = is_admin && params.get(\\"preview\\") === \\"general\\";\\r\\n  return user ? <ConditionalPracticeRoom key={user.id} userId={user.id}\\r\\n    generalUrl={preview ? `/activities/preview/${GENERAL_ID}` : general ? `/activities/${general.id}` : is_admin ? `/activities/preview/${GENERAL_ID}` : undefined}\\r\\n    generalStatus={loading ? \\"Loading your original activity…\\" : error ? \\"Could not load your assignments. Reload to open the original drill.\\" : \\"Your instructor can assign General Conditionals.\\"} /> : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Dev-only lab also uses the real UI and local persistence, never student rows. */\\r\\nexport function ConditionalPracticeLab() {\\r\\n  return <ConditionalPracticeRoom userId=\\"conditional-development-preview\\" localOnly generalStatus=\\"The existing assigned drill opens from the live activity hub.\\" />;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function ConditionalPracticeRoom({ userId, generalUrl, generalStatus, localOnly = false }: {\\r\\n  userId: string; generalUrl?: string; generalStatus?: string; localOnly?: boolean;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();\\r\\n  const [runs, setRuns] = useState<PracticeRun[]>([]);\\r\\n  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [storageError, setStorageError] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [saveStatus, setSaveStatus] = useState(localOnly ? \\"Preview: saved on this device\\" : \\"Loading saved practice…\\");\\r\\n  const [mode, setMode] = useState<ConditionalMode | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [level, setLevel] = useState<\\"all\\" | \\"variables\\" | \\"words\\">(\\"all\\");\\r\\n  const [reviewId, setReviewId] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const alive = useRef(true);\\r\\n  const syncCount = useRef(0);\\r\\n  const syncFailure = useRef<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const run = runs.find(r => r.id === params.get(\\"run\\"));\\r\\n  const selectedWorld = WORLDS.find(w => w.id === mode);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      setRuns(readCachedRuns(userId));\\r\\n      setLoaded(true);\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      if (alive.current) { setLoaded(true); setStorageError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : \\"Could not read saved practice on this device.\\"); }\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (localOnly) return;\\r\\n    let syncError: unknown;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      await syncPendingPractice(userId);\\r\\n    } catch (error) { syncError = error; }\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const saved = await loadPracticeRuns(userId);\\r\\n      if (alive.current) { setRuns(saved); setSaveStatus(syncError ? practiceSaveMessage(syncError) : \\"Saved to your account\\"); }\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      if (alive.current) setSaveStatus(practiceSaveMessage(error));\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }, [userId, localOnly]);\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    alive.current = true;\\r\\n    void refresh();\\r\\n    const online = () => void refresh();\\r\\n    window.addEventListener(\\"online\\", online);\\r\\n    return () => { alive.current = false; window.removeEventListener(\\"online\\", online); };\\r\\n  }, [refresh]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const persist = (next: PracticeRun): boolean => {\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      cachePracticeRun(next);\\r\\n      setRuns(previous => [next, ...previous.filter(r => r.id !== next.id)].sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt)));\\r\\n      setStorageError(null);\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      setStorageError(practiceSaveMessage(error));\\r\\n      return false;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (!localOnly) {\\r\\n      if (syncCount.current === 0) syncFailure.current = null;\\r\\n      syncCount.current++;\\r\\n      setSaveStatus(\\"Saved on this device; syncing…\\");\\r\\n      void syncPracticeRun(userId, next.id).catch(error => { syncFailure.current = practiceSaveMessage(error); })\\r\\n        .finally(() => {\\r\\n          syncCount.current--;\\r\\n          if (alive.current && syncCount.current === 0) setSaveStatus(syncFailure.current ?? \\"Saved to your account\\");\\r\\n        });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return true;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  const update = (change: Partial<PracticeRun>) => run && persist({ ...run, ...change, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });\\r\\n  const start = (world: ConditionalMode, questions?: ConditionalQuestion[]) => {\\r\\n    const selected = questions ?? CONDITIONAL_QUESTIONS.filter(q => q.mode === world && (level === \\"all\\" || q.level === level));\\r\\n    if (!selected.length) return;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const next = createPracticeRun(userId, world, selected);\\r\\n      if (persist(next)) { setParams({ run: next.id }); setMode(null); setReviewId(null); }\\r\\n    } catch { setStorageError(\\"This browser cannot save a new session. Check device storage before starting.\\"); }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  const returnToHub = () => { setParams({}); setMode(null); setReviewId(null); };\\r\\n  const review = runs.find(r => r.id === reviewId);\\r\\n  const current = run?.questions[run.position];\\r\\n  const questionAnswers = run && current ? run.answers.filter(a => a.questionId === current.id) : [];\\r\\n  const last = questionAnswers.at(-1);\\r\\n  const resolved = !!last && (last.verdict === \\"correct\\" || last.revealed || questionAnswers.length >= 2);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const submit = (reveal = false) => {\\r\\n    if (!run || !current || (!run.selection && !reveal) || run.stage === \\"feedback\\" && !reveal) return;\\r\\n    const selected = current.choices.find(c => c.id === run.selection);\\r\\n    const answer = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), questionId: current.id, choiceId: selected?.id,\\r\\n      text: reveal ? \\"Answer revealed\\" : selected!.text,\\r\\n      verdict: reveal ? null : selected!.id === current.correctChoiceId ? \\"correct\\" as const : \\"incorrect\\" as const,\\r\\n      attempt: questionAnswers.length + 1, revealed: reveal, at: new Date().toISOString() };\\r\\n    update({ answers: [...run.answers, answer], stage: \\"feedback\\" });\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  return <main className=\\"conditional-room activity-centre\\">\\r\\n    <div className=\\"conditional-shell\\">\\r\\n      <header className=\\"conditional-topbar\\">\\r\\n        {run || mode || review ? <button onClick={returnToHub} className=\\"conditional-back\\"><ArrowLeft size={16} /> Conditional worlds</button>\\r\\n          : <Link to=\\"/activities\\" className=\\"conditional-back\\"><ArrowLeft size={16} /> Activities</Link>}\\r\\n        <div className=\\"conditional-save\\" role=\\"status\\">{saveStatus}\\r\\n          {!localOnly && saveStatus !== \\"Saved to your account\\" && !saveStatus.includes(\\"syncing\\") && <button onClick={() => void refresh()}>Retry sync</button>}</div>\\r\\n      </header>\\r\\n      {storageError && <p role=\\"alert\\" className=\\"conditional-error\\">{storageError}</p>}\\r\\n      {!loaded ? <p>Loading saved practice…</p> : params.get(\\"run\\") && !run ? <section className=\\"conditional-panel\\"><h1>Session not available</h1><p>Check your connection and account, then retry loading your saved practice.</p><button onClick={() => void refresh()}>Retry</button></section>\\r\\n      : review ? <PracticeReview run={review} onBack={() => setReviewId(null)} />\\r\\n      : run && run.stage === \\"done\\" ? <section className=\\"conditional-panel conditional-summary\\">\\r\\n          <span className=\\"conditional-eyebrow\\">{titleFor(run.mode)}</span><h1>Practice complete</h1>\\r\\n          <p>{firstTryCorrect(run)} of {run.questions.length} correct on the first answer. Corrections and reveals are kept separately.</p>\\r\\n          <div className=\\"conditional-actions\\"><button className=\\"conditional-primary\\" onClick={() => setReviewId(run.id)}>Review answers</button>\\r\\n          <button onClick={() => start(run.mode as ConditionalMode, run.questions)}>Practice again</button><button onClick={returnToHub}>Choose a world</button></div>\\r\\n        </section>\\r\\n      : run && current ? <>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"conditional-heading\\"><span className=\\"conditional-eyebrow\\">{titleFor(run.mode)}</span><span>Question {run.position + 1} of {run.questions.length}</span></div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"conditional-progress\\"><span style={{ width: `${run.position / run.questions.length * 100}%` }} /></div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"conditional-question-grid\\">\\r\\n          <section className=\\"conditional-premises\\"><span className=\\"conditional-eyebrow\\">Given</span>\\r\\n            {current.premises.map((p, i) => <div className=\\"conditional-premise\\" key={i}><span className=\\"conditional-premise-number\\">{i + 1}</span><p><ConditionalNotation text={p} /></p></div>)}\\r\\n            {current.conclusion && <div className=\\"conditional-target\\"><span className=\\"conditional-eyebrow\\">Conclusion</span><p><ConditionalNotation text={current.conclusion} /></p></div>}\\r\\n            <p className=\\"conditional-paper-note\\">Diagram the relationships on paper before opening the choices.</p>\\r\\n          </section>\\r\\n          <section className=\\"conditional-panel conditional-answer\\"><h1>{run.mode === \\"must-be-true\\" ? \\"What must be true?\\" : \\"What\'s the missing link?\\"}</h1>\\r\\n            <p>{run.mode === \\"must-be-true\\" ? \\"Choose the statement that follows from the given rules and facts.\\" : \\"Choose the additional conditional that makes the conclusion follow.\\"}</p>\\r\\n            {run.stage === \\"paper\\" ? <div className=\\"conditional-paper-step\\"><BookOpen size={28} /><h2>Work it out on paper</h2><p>{run.mode === \\"must-be-true\\" ? \\"Follow the arrows. Keep track of what the facts establish.\\" : \\"Start at the conclusion\'s sufficient condition. Find the gap before its necessary condition.\\"}</p><button className=\\"conditional-primary\\" onClick={() => update({ stage: \\"choices\\" })}>I\'ve diagrammed it <ArrowRight size={16} /></button></div>\\r\\n            : <>\\r\\n              <div role=\\"radiogroup\\" aria-label=\\"Answer choices\\" className=\\"conditional-choices\\">{current.choices.map((choice, i) => <label key={choice.id} className={`conditional-choice ${run.selection === choice.id ? \\"selected\\" : \\"\\"}`}>\\r\\n                <input type=\\"radio\\" name={`answer-${current.id}`} checked={run.selection === choice.id} disabled={run.stage === \\"feedback\\"} onChange={() => update({ selection: choice.id })} />\\r\\n                <span className=\\"conditional-choice-letter\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\">{String.fromCharCode(65 + i)}</span><span><ConditionalNotation text={choice.text} /></span>\\r\\n              </label>)}</div>\\r\\n              {run.stage === \\"choices\\" && <button className=\\"conditional-primary\\" disabled={!run.selection} onClick={() => submit()}>Check answer</button>}\\r\\n              {run.stage === \\"feedback\\" && last && <div role=\\"status\\" className=\\"conditional-feedback\\">\\r\\n                <h2>{last.revealed ? \\"Answer revealed\\" : last.verdict === \\"correct\\" ? \\"Correct\\" : \\"Not yet\\"}</h2>\\r\\n                {resolved ? <><div className=\\"conditional-credited\\"><span className=\\"conditional-eyebrow\\">{run.mode === \\"must-be-true\\" ? \\"What follows\\" : \\"The missing link\\"}</span><p><ConditionalNotation text={current.choices.find(c => c.id === current.correctChoiceId)!.text} /></p></div>\\r\\n                  <p className=\\"conditional-explanation\\"><ConditionalNotation text={current.explanation} /></p>\\r\\n                  <button className=\\"conditional-primary\\" onClick={() => update(run.position + 1 === run.questions.length ? If C occurs, the rules do not establish A.\\" : \\"To reach A → D, add C → D. The result is A → B → C → D.\\"}</p></div>\\r\\n        <label className=\\"conditional-select\\">Practice with <select value={level} onChange={e => setLevel(e.target.value as typeof level)}><option value=\\"all\\">All questions, in progression</option><option value=\\"variables\\">Variables</option><option value=\\"words\\">Words and phrases</option></select></label>\\r\\n        <p>{CONDITIONAL_QUESTIONS.filter(q => q.mode === mode && (level === \\"all\\" || q.level === level)).length} questions. You can leave and continue later. <>{generalUrl ? <button className=\\"conditional-primary\\" onClick={() => navigate(generalUrl)}>Open original drill <ArrowRight size={16} /></button> : <p className=\\"conditional-unavailable\\">{generalStatus}</p>}\\r\\n              {unfinished?.general && <button onClick={() => navigate(`${unfinished.general.sourceUrl}?practiceRun=${unfinished.id}`)}>Continue saved practice</button>}</>\\r\\n              : <><button className=\\"conditional-primary\\" onClick={() => { setMode(world.id); setLevel(\\"all\\"); }}>Choose practice <ArrowRight size={16} /></button>\\r\\n                {unfinished && <button onClick={() => setParams({ run: unfinished.id })}>Continue question {unfinished.position + 1}</button>}</>}\\r\\n          </section>;\\r\\n        })}</div>\\r\\n        <section className=\\"conditional-history\\"><h2><History size={20} /> Your practice history</h2><p>First answers, corrections, and revealed answers stay with their session.</p>\\r\\n          {runs.length === 0 ? setReviewId(saved.id) : setParams({ run: saved.id })}>{saved.stage === \\"done\\" ? \\"Review\\" : \\"Continue\\"}<ArrowRight size={16} /></button></div>)}\\r\\n        </section>\\r\\n      </>}\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  </main>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction firstTryCorrect(run: PracticeRun) {\\r\\n  return run.questions.filter(q => run.answers.find(a => a.questionId === q.id)?.verdict === \\"correct\\").length;\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction PracticeReview({ run, onBack }: { run: PracticeRun; onBack: () => void }) {\\r\\n  return <section className=\\"conditional-review\\"><button className=\\"conditional-back\\" onClick={onBack}><ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back</button><h1>{titleFor(run.mode)}: session review</h1><p>{run.createdAt.slice(0, 10)} · {firstTryCorrect(run)} correct on the first answer</p>\\r\\n    {run.questions.map((q, i) => <details key={q.id} className=\\"conditional-panel\\"><summary>Question {i + 1} · {run.answers.find(a => a.questionId === q.id)?.verdict === \\"correct\\" ? Your device copy is kept; reload before continuing.\\");\\r\\nclass CacheError extends Error {}\\r\\nclass SyncError extends Error {}\\r\\n// JSONB returns object keys in database order. Compare JSON values, preserving array order.\\r\\nfunction canonical(value: unknown): string {\\r\\n  return JSON.stringify(value, (_key, item) => item && typeof item === \\"object\\" && !Array.isArray(item)\\r\\n    ? Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(item).sort().map(key => [key, item[key]])) : item);\\r\\n}\\r\\n// Keep the new relation typed until the generated database schema is refreshed.\\r\\ntype PracticeDatabase = {\\r\\n  public: {\\r\\n    Tables: {\\r\\n      conditional_practice_runs: {\\r\\n        Row: { [Key in keyof RunRow]: RunRow[Key] };\\r\\n        Insert: RunRow;\\r\\n        Update: Partial<Pick<RunRow, \\"state\\" | \\"revision\\">>;\\r\\n        Relationships: [];\\r\\n      };\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    Views: Record<string, never>;\\r\\n    Functions: Record<string, never>;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n};\\r\\nconst table = () => (supabase as unknown as SupabaseClient<PracticeDatabase>).from(\\"conditional_practice_runs\\");\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function readCachedRuns(userId: string): PracticeRun[] {\\r\\n  const runs: PracticeRun[] = [];\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {\\r\\n      const key = localStorage.key(i);\\r\\n      if (!key?.startsWith(`${prefix}${userId}:`)) continue;\\r\\n      const cached = readCache(userId, key.slice(`${prefix}${userId}:`.length));\\r\\n      if (cached) runs.push(cached.run);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  } catch (error) {\\r\\n    if (error instanceof CacheError) throw error;\\r\\n    throw new CacheError(\\"Device storage could not be read. Existing saved copies have been kept; retry before continuing.\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return runs.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt));\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction readCache(userId: string, id: string): CachedRun | null {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(keyFor(userId, id));\\r\\n    if (!raw) return null;\\r\\n    const cached = JSON.parse(raw) as CachedRun;\\r\\n    if (cached.run?.userId !== userId || cached.run.id !== id || !Array.isArray(cached.run.answers)) {\\r\\n      throw new CacheError(\\"A saved practice session could not be read. Its stored copy has been kept.\\");\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return cached;\\r\\n  } catch (error) {\\r\\n    if (error instanceof CacheError) throw error;\\r\\n    throw new CacheError(\\"A saved practice session could not be read. Its stored copy has been kept.\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction writeCache(cached: CachedRun) {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    localStorage.setItem(keyFor(cached.run.userId, cached.run.id), JSON.stringify(cached));\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    throw new CacheError(\\"Device storage could not save this change. ## assistant

Two high-priority persistence findings:

1. **Delayed history load can erase a newly acknowledged local answer.** `loadPracticeRuns()` replaces clean cache entries without comparing revisions. A stale SELECT response arriving after a newer upload can regress cached state. Skip remote rows older than the local revision.

2. **Cross-device conflicts cannot recover through the recommended reload.** Conflicted runs stay dirty, subsequent uploads keep failing, and history loading always skips them. Preserve the divergent work through an explicit recovery/fork flow rather than instructing a reload that cannot resolve it.

No further high-priority findings in routing, migration access policies, question explanations, or styles. No files changed.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"47de81","wall_time_seconds":0.5449715,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1196,"output":"{\\r\\n  \\"slug\\": \\"conditional-rephrase\\",\\r\\n  \\"title\\": \\"Conditional Rephrase\\",\\r\\n  \\"subtitle\\": \\"Diagram it on paper, then choose the matching diagram\\",\\r\\n  \\"item_format\\": \\"mcq\\",\\r\\n  \\"instructions\\": {\\r\\n    \\"coach\\": {\\r\\n      \\"drill\\": \\"a conditional-translation drill: the student rewrites a statement as If [sufficient], then [necessary] and gives its contrapositive\\",\\r\\n      \\"rubric\\": [\\r\\n        \\"Two forms are credited and they are the same statement: the if-then form, and its contrapositive. A student who gives either one has translated correctly. On an ordinary one-way conditional, nothing else counts as a direction: the converse (swapping the two sides) and the inverse (negating both without swapping) are errors, not alternative phrasings. Biconditional items are the stated exception, covered below.\\",\\r\\n        \\"The core judgment is which condition is sufficient and which is necessary. Words like if, when, whenever, every, all and the only mark the sufficient side. Words like only if, only when, unless, without, except, requires, presupposes, depends on, necessitates and must mark the necessary side.\\",\\r\\n        \\"Compound conditions (both X and Y) and internal contrasts (X rather than Y) must be carried into the answer whole, not simplified away.\\",\\r\\n        \\"The compound flip is a correctness rule, not a stylistic one. Contraposing \\\\\\"If A and B, then C\\\\\\" yields \\\\\\"If not C, then not A or not B\\\\\\". A student who carries the and through unchanged has written a stronger claim than the statement supports, and that is an error to name, not a phrasing quibble. The same applies in reverse: an or on the trigger side becomes an and.\\",\\r\\n        \\"Two errors have names worth using in the feedback, and both apply to one-way conditionals, not to biconditionals. Reversing the arrow is treating the necessary condition as if meeting it guaranteed the trigger. Negating the trigger is concluding that because the trigger did not fire, the outcome cannot happen. Say which one the student made.\\",\\r\\n        \\"Unless, until and without translate as if not on the trigger side. When the idea being negated there is itself a compound, the and or or inside it flips too.\\",\\r\\n        \\"With no and none the negation attaches to the necessary side, never to the trigger. The subject of the sentence still goes on the if side, intact.\\",\\r\\n        \\"If and only if and then and only then are biconditionals, where the arrow genuinely runs both ways and the converse is therefore credited. Only on these items. A student may write both conditionals, or a single two-way statement, or say that the two sides stand or fall together. Do not credit an exclusive either-or, which says something different. Otherwise is not a biconditional; it means if the first alternative does not happen, the stated consequence follows.\\",\\r\\n        \\"Wording is free. Judge the logical structure, not the phrasing.\\"\\r\\n      ],\\r\\n      \\"partial_rule\\": \\"Mark \'partial\' when the student produces a well-formed conditional but reverses sufficiency and necessity, drops a negation, or trims a compound or contrasting condition down to one part. Mark \'incorrect\' when the response is not a conditional at all, or states a relationship the statement does not support.\\"\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    \\"tagline\\": \\"Find the trigger word, and it tells you which side is which.\\",\\r\\n    \\"task\\": \\"Diagram the statement on paper first. Then choose the one diagram that expresses the complete statement. Your contrapositive is equivalent even when it looks different from the credited diagram.\\",\\r\\n    \\"rules\\": [\\r\\n      \\"Some words introduce the sufficient condition and some introduce the necessary one. That is the whole game.\\",\\r\\n      \\"If, when, whenever, every, all, the only: these mark the sufficient side, the trigger.\\",\\r\\n      \\"Only if, only when, unless, without, requires, depends on, must: these mark the necessary side, what is required.\\",\\r\\n      \\"A necessary condition is required but never a guarantee. It can be met and the thing still not happen.\\",\\r\\n      \\"Unless, until and without all mean the same thing. Read them as if not, or ask yourself what is being required and put that on the then side.\\",\\r\\n      \\"With no and none, the negation belongs to the second half. No X does Y becomes: if it is an X, then it does not do Y.\\",\\r\\n      \\"Contrapositive: negate both sides and swap them.\\",\\r\\n      \\"When you contrapose a compound, and becomes or, and or becomes and. If a thing needs both X and Y, then losing either one alone is enough to sink it.\\",\\r\\n      \\"Two moves are always wrong. Running the arrow backwards, and deciding that because the trigger did not fire the outcome cannot happen.\\",\\r\\n      \\"If and only if and then and only then make the arrow run both ways. Otherwise means if not; it does not make the converse valid.\\"\\r\\n    ],\\r\\n"}'}]

When\\r\\n   * it returns true the item is correct, decided here, with no model call and\\r\\n   * no spinner. Absent for Activities whose answers have no authored structure,\\r\\n   * which then judge exactly as they did before.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  fastPath?: (item: ActivityItem, response: string) => boolean;\\r\\n  /** A world\'s authoritative local judge. When present, the Coach is never called. */\\r\\n  localJudge?: (item: ActivityItem, response: string) => CoachVerdict;\\r\\n  /** Paper-first, deterministic choices supplied by the Conditional Rephrase world. */\\r\\n  paperChoices?: PaperDiagramChoice[];\\r\\n  /** The world\'s structural read of the answer, recorded as telemetry only. */\\r\\n  shadowFault?: (item: ActivityItem, response: string) => string | null;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Attempts already spent on this item in an earlier sitting, replayed from\\r\\n   * the attempt log. Zero for an item being met for the first time. The next\\r\\n   * submit continues the numbering rather than restarting it, so the student\\r\\n   * neither gets a spent attempt back nor writes a duplicate `attempt_no`.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  initialAttempts?: number;\\r\\n  /** What they had written when they left, so a half-typed answer survives. */\\r\\n  initialValue?: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Where to cache what they are typing, before it is ever submitted. Until\\r\\n   * now text only reached storage as part of a submitted attempt, so closing\\r\\n   * the tab mid-sentence on question 24 lost the sentence.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  draftKey?: string;\\r\\n  /** Fired once, when the item is resolved (correct, revealed, or coach failure). */\\r\\n  onResolved: (order: number, finalVerdict: Verdict | null) => void;\\r\\n  /** Append-only attempt record; best-effort, errors are toasted not thrown. */\\r\\n  onRecord: (args: {\\r\\n    itemOrder: number;\\r\\n    responseText: string;\\r\\n    verdict: Verdict | null;\\r\\n    attemptNo: number;\\r\\n    judgeSource?: JudgeSource;\\r\\n    shadowFault?: string | null;\\r\\n    latencyMs?: number;\\r\\n  }) => Promise<void>;\\r\\n  onNext: () => void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Per-item state machine: submit -> coach verdict -> (retry up to cap) ->\\r\\n * reveal credited answer -> next. Owns its own state; the runner remounts it\\r\\n * per item via a key so state resets cleanly.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function ActivityItemView({\\r\\n  item,\\r\\n  format,\\r\\n  isLast,\\r\\n  coach,\\r\\n  answerLabel,\\r\\n  answerPlaceholder,\\r\\n  creditedSecondaryLabel,\\r\\n  promptInWorld = false,\\r\\n  fastPath,\\r\\n  localJudge,\\r\\n  paperChoices,\\r\\n  shadowFault,\\r\\n  initialAttempts = 0,\\r\\n  initialValue = \\"\\",\\r\\n  draftKey,\\r\\n  onResolved,\\r\\n  onRecord,\\r\\n  onNext,\\r\\n}: ActivityItemViewProps) {\\r\\n  const { judge, judging } = useActivityCoach();\\r\\n  // A saved free-text draft from the former version is not one of the five\\r\\n  // diagrams. Ignore it instead of enabling Check with no option selected.\\r\\n  const [value, setValue] = useState(() =>\\r\\n    paperChoices?.length && !paperChoices.some((choice) => choice.id === initialValue)\\r\\n      ? \\"\\"\\r\\n      : initialValue,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const answerRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Debounced rather than per-keystroke: a draft is not durable state and does\\r\\n  // not deserve a write on every character.\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!draftKey || paperChoices?.length) return;\\r\\n    const t = setTimeout(() => saveDraft(draftKey, value), 400);\\r\\n    return () => clearTimeout(t);\\r\\n  }, [draftKey, paperChoices, value]);\\r\\n  const [attempts, setAttempts] = useState(initialAttempts);\\r\\n  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ verdict: FeedbackState; feedback: string; covered?: number[] } | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [resolved, setResolved] = useState(false);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const record = (args: Parameters<ActivityItemViewProps[\\"onRecord\\"]>[0]) => {\\r\\n    // The attempt is the durable record now; the draft has done its job.\\r\\n    if (draftKey) clearDraft(draftKey);\\r\\n    void onRecord(args);\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const resolve = (finalVerdict: Verdict | null, reveal: boolean) => {\\r\\n    setRevealed(reveal);\\r\\n    setResolved(true);\\r\\n    onResolved(item.order, finalVerdict);\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const handleSubmit = async () => {\\r\\n    const attemptNo = attempts + 1;\\r\\n    const startedAt = performance.now();\\r\\n    const since = () => Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Conditional Rephrase records the diagram the student says is on their\\r\\n    // paper. The option already carries its formal verdict, so there is no\\r\\n    // prose interpretation and no possible Coach call.\\r\\n    if (paperChoices?.length) {\\r\\n      const choice = paperChoices.find((candidate) => candidate.id === value);\\r\\n      if (!choice) return;\\r\\n      const verdict = { verdict: choice.verdict, feedback: choice.feedback };\\r\\n      setAttempts(attemptNo);\\r\\n      setResult(verdict);\\r\\n      record({\\r\\n        itemOrder: item.order,\\r\\n        responseText: choice.responseText,\\r\\n        verdict: choice.verdict,\\r\\n        attemptNo,\\r\\n        judgeSource: \\"fast-path\\",\\r\\n        shadowFault: shadowFault?.(item, choice.responseText) ?? null,\\r\\n        latencyMs: since(),\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      if (choice.verdict === \\"correct\\") {\\r\\n        resolve(\\"correct\\", false);\\r\\n      } else if (attemptNo >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {\\r\\n        resolve(choice.verdict, true);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Some Activities describe a closed formal system that can be judged\\r\\n    // locally. Conditional Rephrase is one: direction, polarity, clause\\r\\n    // coverage and the contrapositive are all checkable against the authored\\r\\n    // routing. A local judge is authoritative, including when it says partial\\r\\n    // or incorrect, so this branch never spends an AI call.\\r\\n    if (localJudge) {\\r\\n      const verdict = localJudge(item, value);\\r\\n      setAttempts(attemptNo);\\r\\n      setResult(verdict);\\r\\n      record({\\r\\n        itemOrder: item.order,\\r\\n        responseText: value,\\r\\n        verdict: verdict.verdict,\\r\\n        attemptNo,\\r\\n        judgeSource: \\"fast-path\\",\\r\\n        shadowFault: shadowFault?.(item, value) ?? null,\\r\\n        latencyMs: since(),\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      if (verdict.verdict === \\"correct\\") {\\r\\n        resolve(\\"correct\\", false);\\r\\n      } else if (attemptNo >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {\\r\\n        resolve(verdict.verdict, true);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    // The author already wrote this answer down, so recognising it needs no\\r\\n    // model. Only an exact structural match against a credited answer takes\\r\\n    // this path, which is why it can be trusted: it confirms the answer key\\r\\n    // rather than reasoning about it. Everything else falls through and is\\r\\n    // judged exactly as before.\\r\\n    if (fastPath?.(item, value)) {\\r\\n      setAttempts(attemptNo);\\r\\n      setResult({ verdict: \\"correct\\", feedback: \\"\\" });\\r\\n      record({\\r\\n        itemOrder: item.order,\\r\\n        responseText: value,\\r\\n        verdict: \\"correct\\",\\r\\n        attemptNo,\\r\\n        judgeSource: \\"fast-path\\",\\r\\n        shadowFault: shadowFault?.(item, value) ?? null,\\r\\n        latencyMs: since(),\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      resolve(\\"correct\\", false);\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const res = await judge({ item, studentResponse: value, attemptNo, coach });\\r\\n    setAttempts(attemptNo);\\r\\n\\r\\n    if (!res.ok || !res.verdict) {\\r\\n      // Coach failed. The student is never left stuck, so the credited answer is\\r\\n      // revealed and the item ends -- but it ends UNJUDGED. It used to end as\\r\\n      // \\"incorrect\\", which put a red verdict on an answer nobody had read and\\r\\n      // then scored it as a zero for the rest of time.\\r\\n      toast({\\r\\n        title: \\"Coach unavailable\\",\\r\\n        description: \\"Showing the credited answer so you can keep moving. This one is not counted.\\",\\r\\n        variant: \\"destructive\\",\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      record({\\r\\n        itemOrder: item.order,\\r\\n        responseText: value,\\r\\n        verdict: null,\\r\\n        attemptNo,\\r\\n        judgeSource: \\"fallback\\",\\r\\n        shadowFault: shadowFault?.(item, value) ?? null,\\r\\n        latencyMs: since(),\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      setResult({ verdict: \\"unchecked\\", feedback: \\"\\" });\\r\\n      resolve(null, true);\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const verdict = res.verdict.verdict;\\r\\n    setResult(res.verdict);\\r\\n    record({\\r\\n      itemOrder: item.order,\\r\\n      responseText: value,\\r\\n      verdict,\\r\\n      attemptNo,\\r\\n      judgeSource: \\"coach\\",\\r\\n      shadowFault: shadowFault?.(item, value) ?? null,\\r\\n      latencyMs: since(),\\r\\n    });\\r\\n\\r\\n    if (verdict === \\"correct\\") {\\r\\n      resolve(\\"correct\\", false);\\r\\n    } else if (attemptNo >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {\\r\\n      resolve(verdict, true);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    // else: leave unresolved so the Try again / Show answer affordances show.\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Try again used to empty the box. With a twenty-word answer and a one-word\\r\\n   * miss that is a punishment for being close: the student retypes nineteen\\r\\n   * words they had right. The text stays and arrives selected, so starting over\\r\\n   * is still one keystroke away.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const handleRetry = () => {\\r\\n    setResult(null);\\r\\n    requestAnimationFrame(() => {\\r\\n      const box = answerRef.current;\\r\\n      if (!box) return;\\r\\n      box.focus();\\r\\n      box.select();\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Bailing out to the credited answer ends the item, so the log has to say so.\\r\\n   * It used to write nothing: the replay then saw one sub-cap attempt, called\\r\\n   * the item unresolved, and served it again on the next sitting as though the\\r\\n   * student had never reached it. The row is written at the attempt cap, which\\r\\n   * is what \\"I am done with this one\\" means to the replay, and carries the\\r\\n   * verdict they actually earned so the score is unchanged.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const handleShowAnswer = () => {\\r\\n    // \\"unchecked\\" is not a verdict and must not be written to the log as one.\\r\\n    const shown = result?.verdict;\\r\\n    const verdict = shown && shown !== \\"unchecked\\" ? shown : null;\\r\\n    record({\\r\\n      itemOrder: item.order,\\r\\n      responseText: paperChoices?.find((choice) => choice.id === value)?.responseText ?? value,\\r\\n      verdict,\\r\\n      attemptNo: Math.max(attempts + 1, MAX_ATTEMPTS),\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    resolve(verdict, true);\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"space-y-5\\">\\r\\n      <StatementPanel\\r\\n        item={item}\\r\\n        showQuestionType={attempts > 0}\\r\\n        showPrompt={!promptInWorld}\\r\\n      />\\r\\n      <ItemPrompt\\r\\n        format={format}\\r\\n        item={item}\\r\\n        value={value}\\r\\n        onChange={setValue}\\r\\n        onSubmit={handleSubmit}\\r\\n        answerRef={answerRef}\\r\\n        onRetry={handleRetry}\\r\\n        onShowAnswer={handleShowAnswer}\\r\\n        onNext={onNext}\\r\\n        judging={judging}\\r\\n        attempts={attempts}\\r\\n        result={result}\\r\\n        revealed={revealed}\\r\\n        resolved={resolved}\\r\\n        isLast={isLast}\\r\\n        answerLabel={answerLabel}\\r\\n        answerPlaceholder={answerPlaceholder}\\r\\n        creditedSecondaryLabel={creditedSecondaryLabel}\\r\\n        paperChoices={paperChoices}\\r\\n      />\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Single-question page. Advancing swaps the active item instead of showing a preview stack. */\\r\\nexport function ActivityQuestionStack({\\r\\n  items,\\r\\n  format,\\r\\n  coach,\\r\\n  answerLabel,\\r\\n  answerPlaceholder,\\r\\n  creditedSecondaryLabel,\\r\\n  promptInWorld = false,\\r\\n  fastPath,\\r\\n  localJudge,\\r\\n  paperChoices,\\r\\n  shadowFault,\\r\\n  activeIndex,\\r\\n  resumeAttempts = 0,\\r\\n  resumeValue = \\"\\",\\r\\n  draftKeyFor,\\r\\n  onResolved,\\r\\n  onRecord,\\r\\n  onNext,\\r\\n}: ActivityQuestionStackProps) {\\r\\n  const activeItem = items[activeIndex];\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!activeItem) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    /* Centring the card in a full-screen flex box is right beside the world on\\r\\n       a wide screen and wrong beneath it on a phone, where it added ~700px of\\r\\n       empty space above the question. Below md the card starts where the band\\r\\n       ends. */\\r\\n    <div className=\\"flex items-start px-5 py-6 md:min-h-screen md:items-center md:px-12 md:py-12\\">\\r\\n      <section\\r\\n        key={activeItem.order}\\r\\n        aria-current=\\"step\\"\\r\\n        className=\\"ac-question-card w-full\\"\\r\\n      >\\r\\n        <div className=\\"text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[hsl(var(--aw-eyebrow,var(--ac-emerald-strong)))]\\">\\r\\n          Question {activeIndex + 1}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-4\\">\\r\\n          <ActivityItemView\\r\\n            key={activeItem.order}\\r\\n            item={activeItem}\\r\\n            format={format}\\r\\n            coach={coach}\\r\\n            answerLabel={answerLabel}\\r\\n            answerPlaceholder={answerPlaceholder}\\r\\n            creditedSecondaryLabel={creditedSecondaryLabel}\\r\\n            promptInWorld={promptInWorld}\\r\\n            fastPath={fastPath}\\r\\n            localJudge={localJudge}\\r\\n            paperChoices={paperChoices?.(activeItem)}\\r\\n            shadowFault={shadowFault}\\r\\n            initialAttempts={resumeAttempts}\\r\\n            initialValue={resumeValue}\\r\\n            draftKey={draftKeyFor?.(activeItem.order)}\\r\\n            isLast={activeIndex === items.length - 1}\\r\\n            onResolved={onResolved}\\r\\n            onRecord={onRecord}\\r\\n            onNext={onNext}\\r\\n          />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </section>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

Negation Station uses the\\r\\n * `free-text` renderer (AnswerPanel). `mcq` and `variation` are stubbed for\\r\\n * later Activities so the player itself never needs to change to add a format.\\r\\n */\\r\\ninterface ItemPromptProps extends ComponentProps<typeof AnswerPanel> {\\r\\n  format: ItemFormat;\\r\\n  paperChoices?: PaperDiagramChoice[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function ItemPrompt({ format, paperChoices, ...answerProps }: ItemPromptProps) {\\r\\n  if (paperChoices?.length) {\\r\\n    return <PaperDiagramPanel {...answerProps} choices={paperChoices} />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  switch (format) {\\r\\n    case \\"free-text\\":\\r\\n      return <AnswerPanel {...answerProps} />;\\r\\n    case \\"mcq\\":\\r\\n    case \\"variation\\":\\r\\n    default:\\r\\n      return (\\r\\n        <div className=\\"flex h-full items-center justify-center rounded-[24px] border border-dashed border-border bg-card p-9 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n          This item format is coming soon.\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b16c96","wall_time_seconds":0.2730989,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":345,"output":"import { Fragment } from \\"react\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Keep the visual slash independent of the font\'s combining-glyph support. */\\r\\nexport function ConditionalNotation({ text }: { text: string }) {\\r\\n  return <>{text.split(/([A-Za-z]\\\\u0338)/u).map((part, index) =>\\r\\n    /^[A-Za-z]\\\\u0338$/u.test(part)\\r\\n      ? <span key={index} className=\\"conditional-negation\\" aria-label={`not ${part[0]}`}><span aria-hidden=\\"true\\">{part[0]}</span></span>\\r\\n      : <Fragment key={index}>{part}</Fragment>)}</>;\\r\\n}\\r\\nimport type { Verdict } from \\"../../api/types\\";\\r\\nimport { parseRouting } from \\"./routing\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface DiagramSide {\\r\\n  text: string;\\r\\n  /** Negates the complete authored idea, exactly as a paper diagram would. */\\r\\n  inverted: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface DiagramRoute {\\r\\n  ifSide: DiagramSide;\\r\\n  thenSide: DiagramSide;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PaperDiagramChoice {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  routes: DiagramRoute[];\\r\\n  responseText: string;\\r\\n  verdict: Verdict;\\r\\n  feedback: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction parseRoutes(answer: string): DiagramRoute[] | null {\\r\\n  const parts = answer.split(/;\\\\s*and\\\\s+(?=if\\\\b)/i);\\r\\n  const routes = parts.map((part) => parseRouting(part));\\r\\n  if (routes.some((route) => !route)) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return routes.map((route) => ({\\r\\n    ifSide: { text: route!.ifClause.text, inverted: false },\\r\\n    thenSide: { text: route!.thenClause.text, inverted: false },\\r\\n  }));\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]
